x : nx - 1;\n    const int cy = active ? y : ny - 1;\n\n    const size_t slice = (size_t)nx * (size_t)ny;\n    const Real* col = input + (size_t)cy * nx + cx;  // column base at z == 0\n\n    Real below = col[0];\n    Real center = col[slice];\n\n    for (int z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n        const Real above = col[(size_t)(z + 1) * slice];\n\n        // Stage the current z-plane in shared memory, including a one-element halo.\n        __syncthreads();\n        const Real* plane = input + (size_t)z * slice;\n        tile[ty + 1][tx + 1] = center;\n        if (tx == 0) {\n            tile[ty + 1][0] = plane[(size_t)cy * nx + (cx - 1)];\n        }\n        if (tx == BLOCK_X - 1) {\n            tile[ty + 1][BLOCK_X + 1] = plane[(size_t)cy * nx + min(cx + 1, nx - 1)];\n        }\n        if (ty == 0) {\n            tile[0][tx + 1] = plane[(size_t)(cy - 1) * nx + cx];\n        }\n        if (ty == BLOCK_Y - 1) {\n            tile[BLOCK_Y + 1][tx + 1] = plane[(size_t)min(cy + 1, ny - 1) * nx + cx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const Real left = tile[ty + 1][tx];\n            const Real right = tile[ty + 1][tx + 2];\n            const Real front = tile[ty][tx + 1];\n            const Real back = tile[ty + 2][tx + 1];\n\n            // Simple averaging stencil\n            output[(size_t)z * slice + (size_t)y * nx + x] =\n                (center + left + right + front + back + below + above) / 7.0;\n        }\n\n        below = center;\n        center = above;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);\n    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Device buffers; grid2 starts as a copy of grid1 so that the boundary values (which\n    // the stencil never modifies) are already in place in both buffers.\n    Real* d_grid1 = nullptr;\n    Real* d_grid2 = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_grid1, gridSize * sizeof(Real)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_grid2, gridSize * sizeof(Real)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_grid1, grid1.data(), gridSize * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_grid2, d_grid1, gridSize * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice));\n\n    const dim3 block(BLOCK_X, BLOCK_Y);\n    const dim3 grid(nx > 2 ? (unsigned)(((nx - 2) + BLOCK_X - 1) / BLOCK_X) : 0u,\n                    ny > 2 ? (unsigned)(((ny - 2) + BLOCK_Y - 1) / BLOCK_Y) : 0u);\n    const bool hasInterior = (nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2);\n\n    // Warm up the context so the timed region measures the computation itself.\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n\n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        const Real* in = (iter % 2 == 0) ? d_grid1 : d_grid2;\n        Real* out = (iter % 2 == 0) ? d_grid2 : d_grid1;\n        if (hasInterior) {\n            stencilKernel<<<grid, block>>>(in, out, (int)nx, (int)ny, (int)nz);\n        }\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Copy the result back to the host\n    std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    const Real* d_final = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? d_grid1 : d_grid2;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(finalGrid.data(), d_final, gridSize * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_grid1));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_grid2));\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(finalGrid, \"Grid\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1921,"cache_read_input_tokens":18161,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1921},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2898d874-7b05-40b6-b180-93f71462ab83","uuid":"f17dc077-d3a6-4be4-8988-86b5fb87282c","timestamp":"2026-09-13T20:23:08.411Z","request_id":"req_011Cf24YM5aN3vd5jv7a3qNx"}

Each thread owns one (x, y) column and walks z, keeping `below`/`center`/`above` in registers (each cell is loaded from global memory once instead of three times per column). The current z-plane is staged in a `(BLOCK_Y+2)×(BLOCK_X+2)` shared-memory tile with a one-element halo, so the x/y neighbours come from shared memory. Block size 32×2 after a sweep over configurations.\n- **Boundaries**: the reference copies boundary values through unchanged every iteration, so they're identical in both buffers for the whole run. The device buffers are seeded with one D2D copy up front and the kernel only touches the interior — same result, no per-iteration boundary pass.\n- **Host side**: grid init, argument parsing, validation and `print_results` are unchanged; only H2D/D2H transfers and device allocation were added. The timed region covers the iteration loop plus a `cudaDeviceSynchronize`, matching the original's timed region; transfers sit outside it, like the untimed host initialization.\n- **CMake**: `LANGUAGES CUDA`, `set_source_files_properties(... LANGUAGE CUDA)` so `stencil3d.cpp` is compiled by nvcc, `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native` (overridable), host flags forwarded via `-Xcompiler`. No `--use_fast_math`, to keep results bit-identical.\n\n**Correctness**: I rebuilt the original serial version from the pre-edit source in /tmp and compared `-r` output (sum, min/max, samples, hash) across 11 configurations — 128³, odd sizes (33, 129, 257), non-cubic (100×67×43, 64×8×300), degenerate grids (1, 2, 3, 4×5×6), and 0 iterations. All **bit-identical**. `-v` passes; `-h` and unknown-option handling unchanged.\n\n**Performance** (RTX 3090, vs the serial baseline compiled `-O3 -march=native`):\n\n| case | serial | CUDA |\n|---|---|---|\n| 128³, 10 iters | 39 ms | 1 ms |\n| 256³, 50 iters | 1828 ms | 53 ms (~34×) |\n| 512³, 30 iters | — | 19.5 GCell-updates/s |\n\nOne honest limit: at 512³ the kernel sustains ~19.5 GCUPS while a pure device copy on this GPU reaches the equivalent of ~40 GCUPS, i.e. the stencil runs at roughly half the streaming ceiling. I tried five alternative formulations to close that gap — warp-shuffle neighbours, `double2` vectorized loads, multi-row register blocking, warp-aligned tiles, a plain 3D-block kernel, plus L1 carveout tuning and a block-size sweep — and all landed within ~10% of each other, so this appears to be the memory-system wall for a single-precision-free 7-point stencil here rather than something fixable in the kernel.
